Wick expansion factors out critical action and Hessian determinant, evaluates the higher interaction formally, kills total derivatives and is invariant under formal coordinate changes independently of integral convergence. The selected g solves the stationary equation for tP-X^T X/2. Translation X=g+Y removes its linear part; L=(I-t Hess P(g))^(-1/2) normalizes the quadratic part. The repository proof supplies the exact parameter-dependent identity and every original amplitude. Every exponent map on reflecting hyperplanes constant on group orbits gives a character with prescribed values det(s_H)^(-n_H); its relative invariants are generated by the corresponding product of hyperplane equations. Affine quantizations correspond to flat h-complete algebras with specified classical reduction. Consequently all quantizations of symplectic affine space are isomorphic with the fixed classical marking, since its H2_dR vanishes. This is an inferred corollary, not an endomorphism theorem stated verbatim in BK. Let A=K[x_1,...,x_(2n)] and let f satisfy {f_i,f_j}=J_ij for constant nondegenerate J. The inverse Jacobian is polynomial, so the derivations E_i defined by E_i(f_j)=delta_ij are polynomial and commute: their brackets vanish on all f_j and the df_j are an A-basis. Define star_f=m exp((h/2) sum_ij J_ij E_i tensor E_j). Commutativity of the E_i gives associativity, and its underlying module A[[h]] is flat and h-complete. It has the same first-order Poisson tensor as the original Moyal product, while [f_i,f_j]_(star_f)=h J_ij exactly. On X=Spec A, H1(X,O_X)=H2(X,O_X)=0 gives admissibility and the polynomial Euler homotopy gives H2_dR(X)=0. Period injectivity therefore identifies star_f with the original Moyal quantization. The isomorphism T respects the fixed reduction A and hence T=id modulo h. Its images T(f_i) give the desired polynomial-coefficient formal Weyl frame. No inverse polynomial map f^-1, spatial completion or finite etaleness hypothesis is used. Stacks0EYG--0EYH supplies t=u^e after etale base change, which preserves the normalized divisor valuation and detects the local fractional lattice. The Jacobian is e*u^(e-1), so ordinary trace-dual membership is exactly v_E(a)>=1-e_E. For a regular target derivation transverse to C, D(u)=D(t)/(e*u^(e-1)) has order1-e. [Explicit Formulas and Combinatorial Identities for Generalized Stirling Numbers](https://doi.org/10.1007/s00009-011-0169-x) :: Author manuscript p2 Eq(1.1), recurrence(1.3), generating function(1.4); Theorem2.3 and proof pp5–6. The Hsu–Shiue connection coefficients S(n,k;alpha,beta,r) satisfy S(n+1,k)=S(n,k-1)+(k*beta-n*alpha+r)S(n,k), and their vertical exponential generating function is (1+alpha*t)^(r/alpha)*(((1+alpha*t)^(beta/alpha)-1)/beta)^k/k! for alpha*beta!=0. Parameters may be real or complex. Put c=gamma+d-1, alpha=-d, beta=-1, r=c. Then exactly B_{n,gamma,d}(z)=sum_{k=0}^n S(n,k;-d,-1,c)z^k. Summing the cited recurrence gives B_(n+1)=(z+dn+c)B_n-zB_n', with B_0=1. Summing the vertical generating functions gives precisely (1-dt)^(-c/d)*exp(z*(1-(1-dt)^(1/d))), including the local normalization. Thus the recurrence and named polynomial family are existing generalized-Stirling/Sheffer structure.
